2026-10-10 - Double-shove seam closed: onKnockback's stamp-consume window (400ms) was narrower than the stamp's own settle horizon (700ms in blastForce) — an echo landing 400-700ms late (flap recovery, bad RTT) applied a second impulse on top of the prediction AND the stranded stamp then expired as a false kb-echo. 2026-09-29 - Cap weaponStats key cardinality: mode names (join/switchRoom word), 'w<weaponType>' sources, and death-cell coords are all client-shaped â€” each minted unbounded map keys in memory and in the jsonl rollup forever. Modes cap at 64, sources fold to 'other', cells fold to 'far'.
2026-09-29 - Bound free-form heal claims: team rooms gate heals behind server credit, but non-team rooms accept any claim â€” 25hp at 10/s was a standing invincibility cheat. Heal bucket now 3 per 2s (~37hp/s ceiling, real DPS still out-damages it).
2026-09-29 - Block room hops while dead: reseating runs addPlayer, which spawns alive â€” a dead player could dodge the entire respawn delay by switching rooms.
2026-09-29 - Run teardown on locally-initiated WT socket closes: close() set open=false before the session could signal closed, so die() early-returned and game.onClose never ran â€” kicked/idle-reaped/spectator-evicted WebTransport players stayed registered as zombies forever.
2026-09-29 - Disarm the match clock when a match ends in an empty room: end() always re-armed endsAt, so an unwatched room's countdown burned down forever and a rejoiner inherited a stale mid-match clock. EndsAt=0 re-arms fresh on the first human's tick.
2026-09-29 - Tighten the teleport rate cap from 3/s to 1/1.1s: teleport skips every plausibility check by design, so rate is the only bound â€” and 3/s allowed ~200m/s of untracked warp-hopping. A distance bound is impossible (a thrown warp beacon legally lands hundreds of metres out), so the cap now matches the tightest legit cooldown (blink 1.2s; warp 4s, respawn 3s+).
2026-09-29 - Evict a spectator whose id is claimed by a real join: spectator ids aren't claimed, so a player joining with the same id seated fine but the dispatcher's specRooms check made every one of their messages read-only forever. Eviction runs only after claimIdentity succeeds so a failed join can't kick an innocent spectator.
2026-09-29 - Cap joinWindows at 1024: throttle entries only reset when the same IP rejoins, so unique-IP join floods grew the map forever (its comment claimed self-cleaning).
2026-09-29 - Hibernate CreatureSim with the ball sim: dormant existed and gates both step and interval tick, but nothing ever set it â€” an unwatched room kept spawning and integrating creatures forever, then dumped the grown population on a rejoining player.
2026-09-29 - Drop the flag on a rebalanced carrier: rebalanceTeams can flip a bot flag-carrier onto the flag's own team, which froze the flag in 'carried' forever (tickCarried requires carrier.team !== flag.team and the new own-team can't grab or return it).

2026-10-06 - demo format v6 (types.ts, demoWriter/Reader, FORMAT.md): two durability hardenings from the replay-format best-practice review. (a) projectileEvents gain a trailing u8 targetType — on Hit events it names the victim's lane (ball variant vs HIT_TARGET_NONE), closing the last raw-targetId lookup where a creature/deploy id aliasing a live ball could mis-score the frag; reader gates the 60-byte record on version, older files default 0 = ball lane which their data was anyway. (b) header gains tableHash = crc32 of the persisted enum tables (DEMO_SPECIES/DEPLOY/PICKUP kinds + ball variant arrays) stamped by the writer at serialize — a future table reorder or balance edit now flags old clips via a new demovalidate WARN channel instead of silently remapping ids. Writer stamps it live so a stale caller can't lie. Producer chain updated (recordHit takes the victim lane, impact.ts passes hitBall.variant). demovalidate also checks Fired shooter rosterIdx bounds and Hit lane membership. Verified: new v6 clip validates (formatVersion 6, tableHash stamped, 2815 checks 0 failures) and all existing v5 clips still pass (30k checks 0 failures) — backwards compat holds.
2026-10-06 - full demo audit round 2 — lane-namespace hardening (demo/types.ts, entities/targetIds.ts, server ballSpawn.ts): creatureDemoId masked netId with 0x7fff, so a creature netId over 0x3fff aliased the flag (0xc000) and pickup (0xe000) lanes every 16k spawns — cross-lane clobber in findAliveTargets, recoverMissingSpawns and the seek dead-ball set, which all keyed raw targetId across lanes. Those tables now key targetType*65536+targetId (demoClip.ts, playbackReconstruction.ts + playbackCreatures/Deployables/Targets consumers). creatureDemoId mask tightened to 0x3fff so creatures stay 0x8000-bfff forever; the ball allocator wraps below 0x4000 on both sides (client nextTargetId, server ballSpawn nextId — also stops server ids ever exceeding the u16 file field). Duplicate Spawned at a live ball id used to push a second Ball that never updated or disposed — orphaned frozen mesh; the spawn path now re-keys the existing instance. Deployable shots (weaponType 254, no ownerId) minted targetId=0 — same viewmodel-kick leak class as before; recordFired now attributes them to '[deployable]'.
2026-10-06 - playback audit now covers every lane (demoPlaybackAudit.test.ts, 16 tests): creature proxies must integrate ballistic drift (gravity species fall, mesh tracks pos); deployables spawn a scene mesh, dim on hp 0, pop+dispose on destroy; flags re-pin to the replayed carrier via remotePosAt; pickups spawn/remove; kill events emit a burst; remote replicas must interpolate forward on the real clip (a dead interp freezes all). scrubDemo() replays a whole file through the real lifecycle at 30fps collecting per-lane traces; a creature-bearing crossfire clip (midair_Cannon_4.60s_crossfire.demo, 27 creature + 109 flag + 34 pickup events) exercises the lanes henge lacks.

BoltLauncher/Disc worked by coincidence (wt==slot); every other gun looked up a wrong-or-missing model and rendered invisible hands (Cannon wt14 hit slot 10=Melee). 2026-10-06 - bot head pitch: bots'' look was hard-zeroed every tick (rotation.pitch = 0 / moveState.pitch = 0), so every bot head faced dead level while their shots arced — demos and live matches read as horizontal-only aiming. Heads now track the live aim point: server bots read the held trigger''s aim via arsenal.aimPoint() (botDrive.ts), client bots remember the last fire command''s aim for 1.5s (botBrain.ts locomote), both slewed at the same 5 rad/s wrist turn as yaw and negated into the wire convention (positive pitch = looking down). Disengaged bots ease back to level. Verified on a recorded clip: 521/1872 remote frames carry nonzero pitch, spanning -1.40 (aiming ~80 deg up) to +0.85.
